Build repeated, mirrored and radially arrayed parts so the contract gate passes on the first build. - **c3d-scene-atmosphere**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when a scene_threejs env, baseline or refine session builds the sky, the environment map, clouds or fog. The sky is not a backdrop: it is the light source every PBR material reflects. States what has to be true (scene.environment set before the first frame and agreeing with the visible sky; clouds that read as volumes; haze that leaves the midground readable) and how it is read, with the library's sky, cloud and fog modules as the strong options and the one-line equirect orientation bug that blackens water. ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-scene-atmosphere) · ★410)
- **c3d-scene-composition**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when a scene_threejs session owns the environment, a zone's placement or the camera list, or when scene_frames reported a camera problem. States what the harness's deterministic instruments require of a composition - one height function everything samples, a world that does not end in shot, cameras whose eye, clearance and coverage are measured - and how each is measured, so the brief's own place can satisfy them in its own way. Includes the sky/ground/content classifier that silently decides two gate findings… ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-scene-composition) · ★410)
- **c3d-scene-lighting**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when lighting a scene_threejs baseline, env or refine session, or when scene_frames called a frame too dark, blown out or flat. Light the scene so the frame gate passes and the mood still reads. Gives what the frame gate measures (the exposure band every authored camera has to sit in), the one thing hex colours hide - how dark an albedo really is once the renderer works in linear light - and the sky-lit / practical-lit cases with the starter sunRig as one toolbox. ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-scene-lighting) · ★410)
- **c3d-scene-materials**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use in every scene_threejs build or refine session that authors surfaces - terrain, buildings, props, vegetation. The strongest 'plastic toy' tell is a flat albedo; the second is identical twins. States what the judge reads at crop scale (placeholder_material, flat_ground, monotonous_vegetation, undressed_scene, asset_quality_detail) and what the placement gate measures, then offers short optional practices with the shipped library's real API: surface variance, per-copy variation, roughness, edges near the lens, t… ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-scene-materials) · ★410)
- **c3d-scene-motion**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when a scene_threejs session authors or refines animation, or when scene_frames reported no motion or the judge said nothing_moves. Make the scene visibly move between the two frames the harness photographs, and survive the four mechanisms that silently freeze it. Applies to baseline, zone, refine and compose sessions. Covers how motion is measured, the single update hook the harness actually calls, the amplitudes measured to read at 1024 x 576, and why clearing the motion gate is not the same as scoring anima… ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-scene-motion) · ★410)
- **c3d-scene-night**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when a scene_threejs brief says night, dusk, evening, neon, lamplit or moonlit. Night scenes fail two ways: black frames (the gate catches those) and the CHRISTMAS-TREE look - every window a flat glow, light shafts like white tents, twelve competing hues. States what is measured and judged, then the strong options: the library's night windows and neon, glow without postprocessing, fake light pools, the measured shaft ceiling, a wet road with something to mirror, and few light temperatures. ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-scene-night) · ★410)
- **c3d-scene-water**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when a scene_threejs session builds water in any form - pond, lake, harbor, river, ocean, pool, canal, waterfall or fountain. Water is a dielectric, not a mirror: the single most common water bug is metalness, and the second is an environment bake it has nothing to reflect. States what has to be true and how the judge and the motion probe read it, then the shared noise block that keeps terrain, shoreline and scatter agreeing, Gerstner for open water, and foam that reads. ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-scene-water) · ★410)

- **c3d-threejs-forms**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when writing three.js object geometry in src/object.js or src/parts/*.js on the static_object track, in any session kind. Maps each FORM word in your manifest to the three.js geometry maker that produces it, names the traps that make a threejs part silently ship nothing, and states that this language has no boolean/CSG at all, so cavities and shells are built into the profile instead of subtracted. Options, not a recipe: what the part is made of is the brief's. ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-threejs-forms) · ★410)
- **c3d-threejs-shader-traps**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when a scene_threejs session writes a ShaderMaterial or an onBeforeCompile patch, or when shader_preflight reported an error (on the static threejs track a ShaderMaterial is dropped at GLB export — see that contract). Write custom GLSL that survives this harness's shader gate on the first build. Says which mistakes the harness detects statically, which it detects at runtime, which it cannot see at all, and corrects three widely repeated three.js warnings that do not apply to this renderer. ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-threejs-shader-traps) · ★410)
- **c3d-urdf-joints**: Open only when a 3dcode finding or score item names this topic, never up front. use when authoring or repairing an articulated_object's src/robot.urdf - links, joints, axes, limits, clearances - or when joint_sweep fired. Also covers the rest pose in src/model.py, and applies on every repair round that reported joint_sweep or motion_direction. Explains which pose set the sweep gate tests, why a moved-pose overlap is always an ERROR, and the arithmetic that predicts which way positive q pushes a child before you write the axis. ([gaoypeng/3dcodeverse](https://github.com/gaoypeng/3dcodeverse/tree/HEAD/harness/skills/c3d-urdf-joints) · ★410)
